Bessarabian Village Death Records, 184x (R. Drefs)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 17 Jun 1999
             http://www.odessa3.org

        This document may be freely used for personal, nonprofit

        shared with others, provided the header with copyright

        in any form without permission of the copyright owner.

        Copyright 1999, Reuben R. Drefs

        This file contains death records from the Bessarabian villages
        of South Russia for the 184x Decade.  No records are available for 1849.
        This information was compiled by R. Reuben Drefs, coordinator and
        volunteers Elli Wise, Reuben Drefs, Darlene Drefs from the St.
        Petersburg Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS.
        Edited by Marty McMahon.
